# Beetle Glyphs: structure audit

Generated by `node glyphs/tools/audit.mjs` from the built library (engine 2, library 3). 3305 icons. Each icon is listed once, under its first category; the others it counts in are beside it.

Parts are counted from the centrelines for the parametric icons, the app glyphs and the core set (an app glyph drawn in both weights shows its solid too), and from the rounded outline drawing for the four-style set, with its rounded solid beside it. A dot is a full circle of radius 1 or less.

## Totals

| Set | Icons | Lines | Polylines | Rects | Polygons | Circles | Ellipses | Dots | Arcs | Paths |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| parametric | 12 | 13 | 3 | 2 | 6 | 2 | 1 | 0 | 4 | 0 |
| app glyph | 73 | 65 | 23 | 19 | 24 | 15 | 0 | 0 | 1 | 36 |
| core set | 1854 | 3473 | 1087 | 444 | 45 | 526 | 16 | 80 | 0 | 1991 |
| four-style set | 1366 | 2068 | 349 | 267 | 9 | 523 | 0 | 151 | 0 | 2091 |

## Lines against the diagonals

A straight line counts if it is at least 0.75 long and not already flat or upright. For the four-style set every one of its eight drawings is counted.

| Set | Icons with an exact diagonal | 0–2° off: lines (icons) | 2–5° off: lines (icons) | 5–10° off: lines (icons) | 10–15° off: lines (icons) |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| parametric | 3 | 0 (0) | 0 (0) | 3 (2) | 2 (1) |
| app glyph | 20 | 15 (7) | 29 (5) | 20 (6) | 47 (11) |
| core set | 764 | 214 (122) | 79 (42) | 111 (55) | 404 (141) |
| four-style set | 522 | 1058 (90) | 1370 (114) | 2267 (143) | 1614 (114) |

Dots that line up near a diagonal: 28 pairs in 7 icons (core set brain-circuit, core set chart-scatter, core set grip-horizontal, core set grip-vertical, core set grip, core set palette, core set vault).

Every line and dot pair is in `angles.csv`, with its drawing, its ends and how far off it is.
